matched for a segment depending\\n557: // on the match type.\\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\\n559: \\tconstraintMatches := 0\\n560: \\n561: \\tvar reason string\\n562: \\n563: \\tfor _, c := range constraints {\\n564: \\t\\tv := \\t\\t\\t\\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\\n598: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n599: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n600: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\\n601: \\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n602: \\t\\t\\t}\\n603: \\t\\t} else {\\n604: \\t\\t\\t// no match\\n605: \\t\\t\\tswitch segmentMatchType {\\n606: \\t\\t\\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\\n607: \\t\\t\\t\\t// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints\\n608: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n609: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n610: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping to see if we match the next \\t\\t}\\n624: \\n625: \\tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\\n626: \\t\\tif len(constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {\\n627: \\t\\t\\treason = \\\"did not match ANY constraints\\\"\\n628: \\t\\t\\tmatched = false\\n629: \\t\\t}\\n630: \\tdefault:\\n631: \\t\\treason = fmt.Sprintf(\\\"unknown match type %d\\\", segmentMatchType)\\n632: \\t\\tmatched = false\\n633: \\t}\\n634: \\n635: \\treturn matched, reason, nil\\n636: }\\n637: \\n638: func crc32Num(entityID string, salt string) uint {\\n639: \\treturn uint(crc32.ChecksumIEEE([]byte(salt+entityID))) % totalBucketNum\\n640: }\\n641: \\n642: const (\\n643: \\t// totalBucketNum represents how many buckets we can use to determine the consistent hashing\\n644: \\t// distribution and rollout\\n645: \\ttotalBucketNum uint = \\n651: func matchesString(c storage.EvaluationConstraint, v string) bool {\\n652: \\tswitch c.Operator {\\n653: \\tcase flipt.OpEmpty:\\n654: \\t\\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) == 0\\n655: \\tcase flipt.OpNotEmpty:\\n656: \\t\\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) != 0\\n657: \\t}\\n658: \\n659: \\tif v == \\\"\\\" {\\n660: \\t\\treturn false\\n661: \\t}\\n662: \\n663: \\tvalue := \\t\\treturn strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(v), value)\\n672: \\tcase flipt.OpSuffix:\\n673: \\t\\treturn strings.HasSuffix(strings.TrimSpace(v), value)\\n674: \\tcase flipt.OpIsOneOf:\\n675: value)\\n690: \\t}\\n691: \\n692: \\treturn false\\n693: }\\n694: \\n695: func matchesNumber(c storage.EvaluationConstraint, v string) (bool, error) {\\n696: \\tswitch c.Operator {\\n697: \\tcase flipt.OpNotPresent:\\n698: \\t\\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) == 0, nil\\n699: \\tcase flipt.OpPresent:\\n700: \\t\\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) != 0, nil\\n701: \\t}\\n702: \\n703: \\t// \\n752: func matchesBool(c storage.EvaluationConstraint, v string) (bool, error) {\\n753: \\tswitch c.Operator {\\n754: \\tcase flipt.OpNotPresent:\\n755: \\t\\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) == 0, matched for a segment depending\\n557: // on the match type.\\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\\n559: \\tconstraintMatches := 0\\n560: \\n561: \\tvar reason string\\n562: \\n563: \\tfor _, c := range constraints {\\n564: \\t\\tv := zap.String(\\\"property\\\", c.Property), zap.Error(err))\\n588: \\t\\t\\t// don't return here because we want to continue to evaluate the other constraints\\n589: \\t\\t}\\n590: \\n591: \\t\\tif \\t\\t\\t\\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\\n598: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n599: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n600: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\\n601: \\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n602: \\t\\t\\t}\\n603: \\t\\t} else {\\n604: \\t\\t\\t// no match\\n605: \\t\\t\\tswitch segmentMatchType {\\n606: \\t\\t\\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\\n607: \\t\\t\\t\\t// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints\\n608: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n609: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n610: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping to see if we match the next \\t\\t}\\n624: \\n625: \\tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\\n626: \\t\\tif len(constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {\\n627: \\t\\t\\treason = \\\"did not match ANY constraints\\\"\\n628: \\treturn matched, reason, nil\\n636: }\\n637: \\n638: func crc32Num(entityID string, salt string) uint {\\n639: \\treturn uint(crc32.ChecksumIEEE([]byte(salt+entityID))) % totalBucketNum\\n640: \t\t\t\t\t\tmetrics.AttributeFlagType.String(\"variant\"),\n123: \t\t\t\t\t),\n124: \t\t\t\t),\n125: \t\t\t)\n126: \t\t} else {\n127: \t\t\tmetrics.EvaluationErrorsTotal.Add(ctx, 1, \t\t\tfloat64(time.Since(startTime).Nanoseconds())/1e6,\n133: \t\t\tmetric.WithAttributeSet(\n134: \t\t\t\tattribute.NewSet(\n135: \t\t\t\t\tenvironmentAttr,\n136: \t\t\t\t\tnamespaceAttr,\n137: \t\t\t\t\tflagAttr,\n138: \t\t\t\t),\n139: \t\t\t),\n140: \t\t)\n141: \t}()\n142: \n143: \tif flag.DefaultVariant != nil {\n144: \t\tdv, ok := getVariant(flag.GetDefaultVariant(), != nil {\n194: \t\t\t\treturn resp, err\n195: \t\t\t}\n196: \n197: \t\t\tif matched {\n198: \t\t\t\ts.logger.Debug(reason)\n199: \t\t\t\tsegmentKeys = append(segmentKeys, k)\n200: \t\t\t\tsegmentMatches++\n201: \t\t\t}\n202: \t\t}\n203: \n204: \t\tswitch rule.SegmentOperator {\n205: \t\tcase core.SegmentOperator_OR_SEGMENT_OPERATOR:\n206: \t\t\tif segmentMatches < 1 {\n207: \t\t\t\ts.logger.Debug(\"did not match ANY segments\")\n208: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n209: \t\t\t}\n210: \t\tcase core.SegmentOperator_AND_SEGMENT_OPERATOR:\n211: \t\t\tif len(rule.Segments) != segmentMatches {\n212: \t\t\t\ts.logger.Debug(\"did not match ALL segments\")\n213: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n214: \t\t\t}\n215: \t\t}\n216: \n217: \t\tif len(segmentKeys) > 0 {\n218: \t\t\tresp.SegmentKeys = {\n223: \t\t\treturn resp, err\n224: \t\t}\n225: \n226: \t\tvar (\n227: \t\t\tvalidDistributions []*storage.EvaluationDistribution\n228: \t\t\tbuckets            []int\n229: \t\t)\n230: \n231: \t\tfor == nil {\n237: \t\t\t\t\tbucket := int(d.Rollout * percentMultiplier)\n238: \t\t\t\t\tbuckets = append(buckets, bucket)\n239: \t\t\t\t} else {\n240: \t\t\t\t\tbucket := buckets[len(buckets)-1] + int(d.Rollout*percentMultiplier)\n241: \t\t\t\t\tbuckets = append(buckets, bucket)\n242: \t\t\t\t}\n243: \t\t\t}\n244: \t\t}\n245: \n246: \t\t// no distributions for rule\n247: \t\t// match is true rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\n252: \t\t\treturn resp, nil\n253: \t\t}\n254: \n255: \t\tvar (\n256: \t\t\tbucket = crc32Num(r.EntityId, r.FlagKey)\n257: \t\t\t// \t\t\tresp.Match = false\n267: \t\t\treturn resp, nil\n268: \t\t}\n269: \n270: \t\td := validDistributions[index]\n271: \t\ts.logger.Debug(\"matched distribution\", zap.Reflect(\"evaluation_distribution\", d))\n272: \n273: \t\tresp.Match = true\n274: \t\tresp.VariantKey = d.VariantKey\n275: \t\tresp.VariantAttachment = d.VariantAttachment\n276: \t\tresp.Reason = rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\n277: \t\treturn resp, nil\n278: \t} // end rule loop\n279: \n280: \treturn resp, nil\n281: }\n282: \n283: // Boolean evaluates a request for a on the match type.\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\n559: \tconstraintMatches := 0\n560: \n561: \tvar reason string\n562: \n563: \tfor _, c := range constraints {\n564: \t\tv := evalCtx[c.Property]\n565: \n566: \t\tvar (\n567: \t\t\tmatch bool\n568: \t\t\terr   error\n569: \t\t)\n570: \n571: \t\tswitch c.Type {\n572: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n573: \t\t\tmatch = matchesString(c, v)\n574: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_NUMBER_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n575: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesNumber(c, v)\n576: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_BOOLEAN_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n577: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesBool(c, v)\n578: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_DATETIME_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n579: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesDateTime(c, v)\n580: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_ENTITY_ID_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n581: \t\t\tmatch = matchesString(c, entityId)\n582: \t\tdefault:\n583: \t\t\treturn false, reason, errs.ErrInvalid(\"unknown constraint type\")\n584: \t\t}\n585: \n586: \t\tif err != nil {\n587: \t\t\ts.logger.Debug(\"error matching constraint\", zap.String(\"property\", c.Property), zap.Error(err))\n588: \t\t\t// don't return here because we want to continue to evaluate the other constraints\n589: \t\t}\n590: \n591: \t\tif match {\n592: \t\t\t// increase the matchCount\n593: \t\t\tconstraintMatches++\n594: \n595: \t\t\tswitch segmentMatchType {\n596: \t\t\tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\n597: \t\t\t\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\n598: \t\t\t\tbreak\n599: \t\t\tdefault:\n600: \t\t\t\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\n601: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n602: \t\t\t}\n603: \t\t} else {\n604: \t\t\t// no match\n605: \t\t\tswitch segmentMatchType {\n606: \t\t\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\n607: \t\t\t\t// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints\n608: ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// postJSON marshals body and POSTs it to url. {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to marshal request body: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tresp, err := http.Post(url, \"application/json\", bytes.NewReader(b)) //nolint:noctx\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"HTTP POST to %s failed: %v\", url, err)\n\t}\n\treturn resp\n}\n\n// decodeBody decodes resp.Body into v. resp.Body is drained and closed.\nfunc decodeBody(t *testing.T, resp *http.Response, v any) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(v); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to decode response body: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// assertStatus fatally fails if the response status code doesn't match want.\nfunc assertStatus(t *testing.T, resp *http.Response, want int) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tif resp.StatusCode != want {\n\t\tbody, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)\n\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected HTTP %d, got %d; body: %s\", want, resp.StatusCode, body)\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestBooleanEvaluation_Success verifies that a valid boolean flag evaluation\n// request returns HTTP 200 with the expected response fields populated.\nfunc TestBooleanEvaluation_Success(t *testing.T) {\n\tsrv := newEvaluationTestServer(t)\n\tdefer srv.Close()\n\n\treq := evaluationRequest{\n\t\tRequestID:      \"req-001\",\n\t\tNamespaceKey:   \"default\",\n\t\tFlagKey:        \"checkout-v2\",\n\t\tEntityID:       \"user-abc\",\n\t\tContext:        map[string]string{\"plan\": \"pro\", \"region\": \"us-east\"},\n\t\tEnvironmentKey: \"production\",\n\t}\n\n\tresp := {\n\t\tt.Error(\"reason must not be empty\")\n\t}\n\tif result.RequestID != req.RequestID {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"request_id: got %q, want %q\", result.RequestID, req.RequestID)\n\t}\n}\n\n// \"user-xyz\",\n\t\tContext:      map[string]string{},\n\t}\n\n\tresp := postJSON(t, srv.URL+\"/evaluate/v1/boolean\", req)\n\tassertStatus(t, resp, http.StatusOK)\n\n\tvar result
